([fission-ai/openspec](https://github.com/fission-ai/openspec) · 2.5k installs)
- **openspec-explore**: 進入 explore 模式——在動手前或進行中，當使用者想先把想法、問題、需求想清楚時的思考夥伴。只思考不實作，可讀碼可查 codebase 可產 OpenSpec artifact，但不寫應用程式碼。Use when the user wants to think through something before or during a change；觸發：「先想清楚」「探索這個想法」「這個需求怎麼設計」「explore mode」。 ([KerryHuang/kerryhuang-plugins](https://github.com/KerryHuang/kerryhuang-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/frontend-vue/skills/openspec-explore))
- **openspec-explore**: explore モードに入る - アイデアの探索、問題の調査、要件の明確化のための思考パートナー。change の前や最中に、何かをじっくり考え抜きたいときに使う。 ([SugiKent/sugiken-dev-plugin-public](https://github.com/SugiKent/sugiken-dev-plugin-public/tree/HEAD/plugins/openspec/skills/openspec-explore))

- **openspec-quality-plan**: Generates a complete OpenSpec change package containing proposal, design, and task specs. Use this whenever the user asks to create an implementation plan, an OpenSpec package, or convert a research leaf node into a concrete project plan. ([ianshank/Agents](https://github.com/ianshank/Agents/tree/HEAD/skills/openspec-quality-plan) · ★1)
- **openspec-reflect**: Pre-gate self-check for drift detection and philosophy alignment. Use when: preparing for human gate review, checking implementation quality, or detecting scope drift. ([digital-stoic-org/agent-skills](https://github.com/digital-stoic-org/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/openspec/skills/openspec-reflect) · ★20)
- **openspec-replan**: Adaptive refinement for blocked or drifted changes. Use when: implementation is blocked, scope drifts &gt;20%, or direction needs to change. ([digital-stoic-org/agent-skills](https://github.com/digital-stoic-org/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/openspec/skills/openspec-replan) · ★20)
- **openspec-reverse-update-spec**: 根据代码变更反向更新 OpenSpec 四个工件（proposal/design/specs/tasks）。当用户说"反向更新spec"、"反向更新工件"、"我自己修改了代码，更新下spec文档"时使用此技能。支持无输入（分析 Git 变更）或指定文件路径。输出更新建议后必须询问用户确认再执行更新。 ([kinneyyan/prompts](https://github.com/kinneyyan/prompts/tree/HEAD/skills/openspec-reverse-update-spec) · ★2)
- **openspec-review**: Pre-implementation tech lead review gate for OpenSpec changes. Reviews proposal+design+tasks+tests as a whole. Use when: review change, openspec review, tech lead review, architecture review, ready to implement?, pre-implementation check, review before developing. ([digital-stoic-org/agent-skills](https://github.com/digital-stoic-org/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/openspec/skills/openspec-review) · ★20)
- **openspec-review**: Run the full review gate for an implemented OpenSpec change before archive. Invokes code/tests/architecture/frontend reviewers in parallel, consolidates findings, applies fixes, and validates until green. Use after `apply` completes all tasks and before `archive`. ([elevenyellow/OneJs_Boilerplate](https://github.com/elevenyellow/OneJs_Boilerplate/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/openspec-review))
- **openspec-review-changes**: 現在の openspec/changes を全件横断レビューし、change 内・change 間の矛盾、重複、依存漏れ、仕様の齟齬、曖昧な判断を洗い出して、1 フェーズ最大 3 問の質問を再帰的に繰り返し、すべての疑問点を解消する。ユーザーが複数 change の整合性確認、競合確認、実装前レビュー、全 change の疑問解消、まとめて仕様を詰めることに触れたら必ずこのスキルを使う。 ([SugiKent/sugiken-dev-plugin-public](https://github.com/SugiKent/sugiken-dev-plugin-public/tree/HEAD/plugins/openspec/skills/openspec-review-changes))
- **openspec-review-code**: Review staged and unstaged changes before committing to ensure code quality, minimize complexity, and eliminate duplication. ([dougis-org/cookbook-tanstack](https://github.com/dougis-org/cookbook-tanstack/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/openspec-review-code))
- **openspec-roadmap**: Decomposes a PRD or large epic into prioritized OpenSpec slices in openspec/roadmap.md and keeps slice lifecycle in sync with propose, apply, and archive. Use when a PRD exists but work is still one mega proposal, when breaking a feature into multiple OpenSpec changes, when picking the next slice, or when updating roadmap status after OpenSpec steps. ([bardaxx/skillbook](https://github.com/bardaxx/skillbook/tree/HEAD/skills/engineering/openspec-roadmap) · ★3)
- **openspec-setup**: 「OpenSpecを導入して」「この作業ディレクトリでOpenSpecを使えるようにして」 「openspec initして」と言われたとき、npm経由でのopenspec CLI導入確認から openspec init・schemaのローカルfork・claude-harness-kitのルールファイルへの 参照ポインタ埋め込みまでを自動化する。グローバルインストールとファイル生成を 伴うため、各ステップで確認を挟む。 ([kokuto821/claude-harness-kit](https://github.com/kokuto821/claude-harness-kit/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude-harness-kit/skills/openspec-setup) · ★1)
- **openspec-ship**: Use when shipping a completed feature and generating as-built OpenSpec docs before branch finalization ([dkpapadopoulos/auto-claude-skills](https://github.com/dkpapadopoulos/auto-claude-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/openspec-ship) · ★6)
- **openspec-sp-apply**: Implement, continue, or resume an OpenSpec SP Lite change with evidence-driven testing, root-cause debugging, and fresh verification. Use when the user asks to start executing an approved plan, continue pending tasks, resume work, finish a change, diagnose and fix a bug, repair a regression, or address failing tests, builds, or unexpected behavior. ([Wkingxc/openspec-sp-lite](https://github.com/Wkingxc/openspec-sp-lite/tree/HEAD/plugins/openspec-sp-lite/skills/openspec-sp-apply) · ★1)
- **openspec-sp-apply**: 实现、继续或恢复一个变更，并以内层统一证据策略执行根因调试、按需测试先行和新鲜验证。 ([Wkingxc/openspec-sp-lite](https://github.com/Wkingxc/openspec-sp-lite/tree/HEAD/docs/zh-prompt-reference/skills/openspec-sp-apply) · ★1)
- **openspec-sp-archive**: 仅在用户明确要求后，严格验证、同步 delta specs 并归档已完成变更。 ([Wkingxc/openspec-sp-lite](https://github.com/Wkingxc/openspec-sp-lite/tree/HEAD/docs/zh-prompt-reference/skills/openspec-sp-archive) · ★1)
- **openspec-sp-archive**: Validate, synchronize delta specifications, and archive a completed OpenSpec SP Lite change. Use only when the user explicitly asks to finalize or archive a change after implementation and verification are complete. ([Wkingxc/openspec-sp-lite](https://github.com/Wkingxc/openspec-sp-lite/tree/HEAD/plugins/openspec-sp-lite/skills/openspec-sp-archive) · ★1)
- **openspec-sp-explore**: Enter a read-only exploration stance for vague ideas, unclear requirements, architecture questions, trade-offs, or problems that need investigation before or during a change. Use when the user wants to think, compare options, inspect the codebase, or clarify what should be built without implementing it. ([Wkingxc/openspec-sp-lite](https://github.com/Wkingxc/openspec-sp-lite/tree/HEAD/plugins/openspec-sp-lite/skills/openspec-sp-explore) · ★1)
- **openspec-sp-explore**: 对模糊想法、架构问题、方案权衡或尚未查清的问题进入只读探索姿态。用于思考和调查，不实现功能。 ([Wkingxc/openspec-sp-lite](https://github.com/Wkingxc/openspec-sp-lite/tree/HEAD/docs/zh-prompt-reference/skills/openspec-sp-explore) · ★1)
- **openspec-sp-init**: 初始化固定的 OpenSpec SP Lite 产物结构。仅在用户明确要求初始化、设置或接入时使用。 ([Wkingxc/openspec-sp-lite](https://github.com/Wkingxc/openspec-sp-lite/tree/HEAD/docs/zh-prompt-reference/skills/openspec-sp-init) · ★1)
- **openspec-sp-init**: Initialize the fixed OpenSpec SP Lite artifact structure in a project. Use only when the user explicitly asks to initialize, set up, or adopt OpenSpec SP Lite in the current project. ([Wkingxc/openspec-sp-lite](https://github.com/Wkingxc/openspec-sp-lite/tree/HEAD/plugins/openspec-sp-lite/skills/openspec-sp-init) · ★1)
- **openspec-sp-propose**: 把已经足够清晰的问题形式化为可执行的 proposal、需求场景、可选 design 和 tasks。 ([Wkingxc/openspec-sp-lite](https://github.com/Wkingxc/openspec-sp-lite/tree/HEAD/docs/zh-prompt-reference/skills/openspec-sp-propose) · ★1)
- **openspec-sp-propose**: Create an implementation-ready OpenSpec-style change with proposal, requirements and scenarios, optional design, and trackable tasks. Use when the problem and scope are clear enough to formalize, the user asks to propose or plan a change, or an exploration is ready to become persisted artifacts. ([Wkingxc/openspec-sp-lite](https://github.com/Wkingxc/openspec-sp-lite/tree/HEAD/plugins/openspec-sp-lite/skills/openspec-sp-propose) · ★1)
- **openspec-sp-review**: Perform an OpenSpec-aware read-only code review only when the user explicitly invokes this skill or explicitly asks for a code review of a change. Do not use automatically after implementation or verification. ([Wkingxc/openspec-sp-lite](https://github.com/Wkingxc/openspec-sp-lite/tree/HEAD/plugins/openspec-sp-lite/skills/openspec-sp-review) · ★1)
- **openspec-sp-review**: 仅在用户明确调用或明确要求代码审查时，执行结合 OpenSpec 产物的只读审查。 ([Wkingxc/openspec-sp-lite](https://github.com/Wkingxc/openspec-sp-lite/tree/HEAD/docs/zh-prompt-reference/skills/openspec-sp-review) · ★1)
- **openspec-stale**: Check for open OpenSpec changes that may have already been implemented and merged into dev. ([kfitzpatrick/claude-toolkit](https://github.com/kfitzpatrick/claude-toolkit/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/openspec-stale))
- **openspec-status**: Check the status of all open OpenSpec changes — identify stale ones already merged into dev, and surface open PRs that address them. ([kfitzpatrick/claude-toolkit](https://github.com/kfitzpatrick/claude-toolkit/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/openspec-status))
- **openspec-story-claim**: Claim one ready, unclaimed story from an OpenSpec initiative and execute it end-to-end, leaving a clean handoff. Use when starting a fresh session on a new story in an OpenSpec change workspace. ([grzegorznowak/add](https://github.com/grzegorznowak/add/tree/HEAD/claude/skills/openspec-story-claim) · ★1)
- **openspec-story-converge**: Run fresh claim/resume implementation passes against one OpenSpec change until it reaches Status: 🟣 IN REVIEW, becomes blocked, or the loop reaches a hard stop. Use when a plan-approved implementation story needs continuation only; independent review is intentionally left to a separate fresh session. ([grzegorznowak/add](https://github.com/grzegorznowak/add/tree/HEAD/claude/skills/openspec-story-converge) · ★1)
- **openspec-story-driver**: Drive OpenSpec change implementation through a story graph stored as markdown notes in an Obsidian vault. Decompose a change into fully-specified stories (max 3 acceptance criteria each), write them into the vault, then continuously poll for the next runnable story, implement it, compact context, and repeat until the change is complete. ([aplacaba/agent-skills](https://github.com/aplacaba/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/openspec-story-driver))
